 if(s.studyFinance!==undefined){const f=s.studyFinance;if(!r14Object(f)||!r14Int(f.nextId,1,1000)||!Array.isArray(f.loans)||f.loans.length>200||new Set(f.loans.map(l=>l?.id)).size!==f.loans.length||!Array.isArray(f.appeals)||f.appeals.length>4000||!Array.isArray(f.graduateHandled)||f.graduateHandled.length>200||!f.graduateHandled.every(k=>r14Text(k,100)))return false;for(const l of f.loans){if(!r14Object(l)||!/^edu\d+$/.test(l.id)||!r14Country(l.country)||!['college','university'].includes(l.level)||!Object.hasOwn(faculties,l.facultyId)||!Object.hasOwn(l.level==='college'?colleges:universities,l.institution)||!r14Int(l.limit,0,1e9)||!r14Int(l.principal,0,l.limit)||l.rate!==.04||!['studying','grace','repaying','paid'].includes(l.status)||!r14Int(l.approvedAge,0,s.age)||l.graceUntil!==null&&!r14Int(l.graceUntil,0,122)||!r14Int(l.remainingYears,0,7)||!r14Int(l.lastDrawAge,-1,s.age)||!r14Int(l.lastPaymentAge,-1,s.age))return false;}
 for(const a of f.appeals)if(!r14Object(a)||!r14Int(a.parent,0,(s.parents||[]).length-1)||!r14Fields.includes(a.field)||!r14Int(a.age,0,s.age)||typeof a.accepted!=='boolean')return false;
 }
 const m=s.mobility;if(m){if(m.citizenships!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(m.citizenships)||m.citizenships.length>195||!m.citizenships.every(r14Country)||new Set(m.citizenships).size!==m.citizenships.length))return false;if(m.citizenshipHistory!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(m.citizenshipHistory)||m.citizenshipHistory.length>120||!m.citizenshipHistory.every(x=>r14Object(x)&&r14Country(x.from)&&r14Country(x.to)&&r14Int(x.age,0,s.age))))return false;
 if(m.courseYears!==undefined&&(!r14Object(m.courseYears)||!Object.entries(m.courseYears).every(([k,v])=>Object.hasOwn(languageNames,k)&&r14Int(v,0,s.age))))return false;
 if(m.courseSupport!==undefined&&(!r14Object(m.courseSupport)||!r14Int(m.courseSupport.age,0,s.age)||!Array.isArray(m.courseSupport.paid)||m.courseSupport.paid.length>4||!m.courseSupport.paid.every(x=>r14Number(x,0,1e8))))return false;
 if(m.residence!==undefined){const r=m.residence;if(!r14Object(r)||r.country!==s.country||!r14Int(r.since,0,s.age)||!r14Number(r.lawfulYears,0,120)||!r14Number(r.workYears,0,120)||!r14Int(r.permanentSince,-1,s.age)||!r14Int(r.integration,0,100))return false;for(const key of ['prepYear','prAttemptYear','citizenAttemptYear','lastCountAge'])if(!r14Int(r[key],-1,s.age))return false;}
 if(m.arrivalCourse!==undefined&&m.arrivalCourse!==null){const a=m.arrivalCourse;if(!r14Object(a)||!r14Country(a.country)||!Object.hasOwn(languageNames,a.language)||!r14Int(a.completedYears,0,2)||!r14Int(a.lastYear,-1,s.age))return false;}
 }
 if(s.business?.branding){const b=s.business.branding;if(!r14Object(b)||!Object.hasOwn(businessLogoOptions,b.icon)||!Object.hasOwn(businessBrandColors,b.color))return false;}
 for(const e of [s.event,...s.pendingEvents||[]].filter(Boolean))for(const c of e.choices)if(c.effect==='graduate'&&(!['accept','later','decline'].includes(c.response)||!s.recruiterOffers?.some(o=>o.id===c.offerId)))return false;
 return true;
}

const saveFinite=(n,min,max)=>typeof n==='number'&&Number.isFinite(n)&&n>=min&&n<=max;
const saveObject=o=>o!==null&&typeof o==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o);
const saveText=(s,max=100)=>typeof s==='string'&&s.length>0&&s.length<=max;
const saveStatKeys=['health','happy','iq','looks','relation','wanted','reputation','sports','academics','creativity','crimeAffinity'];
const saveSkillKeys=['it','technical','business','education','service','arts','sports','medicine'];
const saveEffects=['life','regional','invest','safe','rest','stress','friend','read','education','invitation','bestfriend','business','assignment','graduate'];
function validSavedEvent(event){if(event===null)return true;if(!saveObject(event))return false;if(event.personRef!==undefined&&!validPersonRef(event.personRef))return false;if(!saveObject(event)||!saveText(event.title,300)||!saveText(event.text,4000)||!Array.isArray(event.choices)||event.choices.length<1||event.choices.length>8)return false;return event.choices.every(c=>{if(!saveObject(c)||!saveText(c.label,500)||!saveEffects.includes(c.effect))return false;if(c.effect==='bestfriend'&&(typeof c.friendId!=='string'||!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(c.friendId)||!['accept','decline'].includes(c.response)))return false;if(c.personRef!==undefined&&!validPersonRef(c.personRef))return false;if(c.effect==='invitation'&&(typeof c.invitationId!=='string'||!/^invite-[0-9]{1,8}$/.test(c.invitationId)||!['accept','decline'].includes(c.response)||c.meetId!==undefined&&!/^n[0-9]{1,8}$/.test(c.meetId)))return false;if(c.familyTarget!==undefined&&(!saveObject(c.familyTarget)||!['parent','relative'].includes(c.familyTarget.type)||typeof c.familyTarget.id!=='string'||!(c.familyTarget.type==='parent'?/^[0-3]$/:/^r[0-9]{1,4}$/).test(c.familyTarget.id)||!saveFinite(c.familyBond,-100,100)))return false;if(c.effect==='education'&&(!['school','delay','route','advice','college','university','pause','later','clubDecline'].includes(c.action)||typeof c.value!=='string'||c.value.length>100))return false;if(c.effect==='education'&&!validEducationChoice(c))return false;if(c.cost!==undefined&&!saveFinite(c.cost,0,1e9))return false;if(['life','regional','education'].includes(c.effect)&&!saveText(c.result,4000))return false;if(c.result!==undefined&&!saveText(c.result,4000))return false;if(c.changes!==undefined&&(!saveObject(c.changes)||!Object.entries(c.changes).every(([key,n])=>[...saveStatKeys,'money'].includes(key)&&saveFinite(n,-1e9,1e9))))return false;if(c.skills!==undefined&&(!saveObject(c.skills)||!Object.entries(c.skills).every(([key,n])=>saveSkillKeys.includes(key)&&saveFinite(n,-100,100))))return false;for(const key of ['bond','trust','otherBond','parentBond','partnerBond','friendBond','performance','sportRep','sportSkill','sportForm','skillCurrent','portfolio'])if(c[key]!==undefined&&!saveFinite(c[key],-100,100))return false;if(c.meet!==undefined&&typeof c.meet!=='boolean')return false;for(const key of ['friendId','otherFriendId'])if(c[key]!==undefined&&(typeof c[key]!=='string'||!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(c[key])))return false;if(c.friendLink!==undefined){const link=c.friendLink;if(!saveObject(link)||typeof link.a!=='string'||typeof link.b!=='string'||!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(link.a)||!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(link.b)||!saveFinite(link.delta,-100,100))return false;}return true;});}
function validateSave(raw){if(!saveObject(raw)||!Number.isInteger(raw.version)||raw.version<1||raw.version>SAVE_VERSION||!Number.isInteger(raw.age)||!saveFinite(raw.age,0,120)||!saveText(raw.name,80)||!saveText(raw.country,2)||!saveFinite(raw.money,-1e12,1e12)||typeof raw.alive!=='boolean')return false;for(const key of ['health','happy','iq','looks'])if(!saveFinite(raw[key],0,100))return false;for(const key of saveStatKeys)if(raw[key]!==undefined&&!saveFinite(raw[key],0,100))return false;if(!Array.isArray(raw.log)||raw.log.length>30000||!raw.log.every(l=>saveObject(l)&&Number.isInteger(l.age)&&saveFinite(l.age,0,raw.age)&&saveText(l.text,4000)&&['normal','chapter','finance','sport','friend','health','life',undefined].includes(l.kind)))return false;if(raw.event!==undefined&&!validSavedEvent(raw.event))return false;if(raw.recentEvents!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(raw.recentEvents)||raw.recentEvents.length>12||!raw.recentEvents.every(id=>saveText(id,100))))return false;if(raw.pendingEvents!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(raw.pendingEvents)||raw.pendingEvents.length>20||!raw.pendingEvents.every(e=>e!==null&&validSavedEvent(e))))return false;

if(raw.sport!==undefined){const sp=raw.sport;const keys=['football','basketball','tennis','swimming','gymnastics','skating','athletics','cycling','boxing','chess'];if(!saveObject(sp)||!saveObject(sp.records)||sp.active!==null&&!keys.includes(sp.active)||!saveFinite(sp.hours,0,32)||!saveFinite(sp.injury,0,5))return false;for(const [key,r] of Object.entries(sp.records))if(!keys.includes(key)||!saveObject(r)||!saveFinite(r.skill,0,100)||!saveFinite(r.reputation,0,100)||!saveFinite(r.form,0,100)||!Number.isInteger(r.tier)||!saveFinite(r.tier,0,5))return false;for(const r of Object.values(sp.records)){for(const key of ['wins','seasons'])if(!Number.isInteger(r[key])||!saveFinite(r[key],0,10000))return false;for(const key of ['reviewYear','competitionYear'])if(!saveFinite(r[key],-1,120))return false;}if(sp.active&&!sp.records[sp.active])return false;for(const r of Object.values(sp.records)){if(r.teamwork!==undefined&&!saveFinite(r.teamwork,0,100))return false;if(r.retired!==undefined&&typeof r.retired!=='boolean')return false;}if(sp.rehab!==undefined&&typeof sp.rehab!=='boolean')return false;}

function validateCitySave(raw){const c=raw.city;if(c===undefined)return true;if(!saveObject(c)||c.residence!==null&&!allHomes.some(h=>h.id===c.residence)||!Array.isArray(c.owned)||c.owned.length>allHomes.length||new Set(c.owned).size!==c.owned.length||c.owned.some(id=>!allHomes.some(h=>h.id===id))||!saveFinite(c.deposit,0,1e9)||!['walk','transit','bike','car'].includes(c.mode))return false;

if(c.partyYear!==undefined&&!saveFinite(c.partyYear,-1,raw.age))return false;if(c.propertyRecords!==undefined){if(!saveObject(c.propertyRecords)||Object.keys(c.propertyRecords).length>allHomes.length||!Object.entries(c.propertyRecords).every(([id,r])=>c.owned.includes(id)&&saveObject(r)&&saveFinite(r.condition,0,100)&&Number.isInteger(r.years)&&saveFinite(r.years,0,120)&&['neglect','standard','careful'].includes(r.care)&&saveFinite(r.repairYear,-1,raw.age)&&saveFinite(r.partyYear,-1,raw.age)))return false;}if(raw.version>=8&&raw.housing==='owned'&&!c.owned.includes(c.residence))return false;return true;}
function validVacancy(v){return saveObject(v)&&saveText(v.id,100)&&/^c[0-9]{1,12}$/.test(v.companyId)&&saveText(v.companyName,120)&&Object.hasOwn(jobTracks,v.jobId)&&Number.isInteger(v.level)&&saveFinite(v.level,0,3)&&saveFinite(v.payFactor,.1,5)&&typeof v.ladder==='boolean'&&['office','remote','hybrid'].includes(v.format)&&saveFinite(v.culture,0,100)&&validWorkLocation(v.location)&&saveText(v.site,30)&&saveFinite(v.posted,0,121)&&saveFinite(v.expires,0,125);}
function validWorkLocation(p){return saveObject(p)&&saveFinite(p.x,0,100)&&saveFinite(p.y,0,100)&&Object.hasOwn(cityDistricts,p.district)&&saveText(p.site,100);}
function validateEmploymentSave(raw){if(raw.professionalCertificates!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(raw.professionalCertificates)||raw.professionalCertificates.length>400||!raw.professionalCertificates.every(c=>saveObject(c)&&Object.hasOwn(professionalCourses,c.course)&&saveSkillKeys.includes(c.sector)&&saveFinite(c.age,0,raw.age))))return false;

}if(raw.work?.stageYear!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(raw.work.stageYear)||!saveFinite(raw.work.stageYear,0,raw.work.tenure)))return false;return true;}

function validEducationChoice(c){if(c.action==='school')return Object.hasOwn(schoolTracks,c.value);if(c.action==='route')return ['upper','college','work'].includes(c.value);if(c.action==='advice')return ['football','computer','science','theatre','volunteer','debate'].includes(c.value);if(c.action==='college'||c.action==='university'){const [id,faculty,...rest]=c.value.split(':');const catalog=c.action==='college'?colleges:universities;return !rest.length&&Object.hasOwn(catalog,id)&&catalog[id].faculties.includes(faculty);}return c.value==='';}

function validateShoppingSave(raw){const q=raw.shopping;if(q===undefined)return true;if(!saveObject(q)||!Array.isArray(q.wardrobe)||q.wardrobe.length>clothingProducts.length||new Set(q.wardrobe).size!==q.wardrobe.length||!q.wardrobe.every(id=>clothingProducts.some(p=>p.id===id))||!Array.isArray(q.electronics)||q.electronics.length>electronicProducts.length||!q.electronics.every(r=>saveObject(r)&&electronicProducts.some(p=>p.id===r.id)&&saveFinite(r.condition,0,100)&&Number.isInteger(r.years)&&saveFinite(r.years,0,120)&&saveFinite(r.useYear,-1,raw.age)&&saveFinite(r.repairYear,-1,raw.age))||new Set(q.electronics.map(r=>r.id)).size!==q.electronics.length)return false;if(q.activeComputer!==null&&!q.electronics.some(r=>r.id===q.activeComputer&&electronicProducts.find(p=>p.id===r.id)?.category==='computer'))return false;return true;}
function validateAffectionSave(raw){for(const p of raw.parents||[])if(p.gifts!==undefined&&(!saveObject(p.gifts)||!saveFinite(p.gifts.normalYear,-1,raw.age)))return false;for(const f of raw.friends||[]){for(const k of ['feelings','compatibility'])if(f[k]!==undefined&&!saveFinite(f[k],0,100))return false;for(const k of ['romanticInterest','romanticBoundary'])if(f[k]!==undefined&&typeof f[k]!=='boolean')return false;if(f.romanceYear!==undefined&&!saveFinite(f.romanceYear,-1,raw.age))return false;if(f.gifts!==undefined&&(!saveObject(f.gifts)||!saveFinite(f.gifts.normalYear,-1,raw.age)||!saveFinite(f.gifts.romanticYear,-1,raw.age)))return false;}return true;}

function validateFamilySave(raw){for(const p of raw.parents||[]){if(p.wealth!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(p.wealth)||!wealthLevels[p.wealth]))return false;if(p.housingTenure!==undefined&&!['owned','rent'].includes(p.housingTenure))return false;if(p.educationPaid!==undefined&&!saveFinite(p.educationPaid,0,1e12))return false;if(p.estateSettled!==undefined&&typeof p.estateSettled!=='boolean')return false;}const f=raw.family;if(f===undefined)return true;if(!saveObject(f)||!['owned','rent'].includes(f.homeTenure)||!allHomes.some(h=>h.id===f.homeId)||typeof f.homeSettled!=='boolean'||!saveFinite(f.educationPaid,0,1e12)||!Array.isArray(f.homeOwners)||f.homeOwners.length>4||new Set(f.homeOwners).size!==f.homeOwners.length||!f.homeOwners.every(i=>Number.isInteger(i)&&i>=0&&i<(raw.parents?.length||0)))return false;return true;}

function validateCreativeSave(raw){if(raw.creative===undefined)return true;const q=raw.creative;if(!saveObject(q)||!saveObject(q.tracks)||Object.keys(q.tracks).length!==3||!Object.keys(creativePaths).every(id=>saveObject(q.tracks[id])))return false;let hours=0;for(const r of Object.values(q.tracks)){if(![0,2,4,8,12].includes(r.hours)||!Number.isInteger(r.level)||!saveFinite(r.level,0,4)||!Number.isInteger(r.years)||!saveFinite(r.years,0,120))return false;for(const key of ['skill','portfolio','reputation'])if(!saveFinite(r[key],0,100))return false;for(const key of ['practiceYear','projectYear','auditionYear'])if(!Number.isInteger(r[key])||!saveFinite(r[key],-1,raw.age))return false;hours+=r.hours;}return hours<=24&&hours===raw.creativeHours;}
function validateMotorSave(raw){const c=raw.city;if(!c)return true;for(const r of c.vehicles){if(r.body!==undefined&&!saveFinite(r.body,0,100))return false;if(r.paint!==undefined&&!Object.hasOwn(carColors,r.paint))return false;if(r.mileage!==undefined&&!saveFinite(r.mileage,0,5000000))return false;for(const key of ['serviceYear','repairYear','paintYear'])if(r[key]!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(r[key])||!saveFinite(r[key],-1,raw.age)))return false;}const m=c.autoMarket;if(m===undefined)return true;if(!saveObject(m)||!Number.isInteger(m.year)||!saveFinite(m.year,0,raw.age)||!Array.isArray(m.stock)||m.stock.length!==25||new Set(m.stock.map(r=>r?.id)).size!==25)return false;for(const dealer of dealerships){const list=m.stock.filter(r=>r?.dealer===dealer.id);if(list.length!==5||new Set(list.map(r=>carModels.find(v=>v.id===r.modelId)?.brand)).size!==5)return false;for(const r of list){const model=carModels.find(v=>v.id===r.modelId);if(!saveObject(r)||!model||!Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>dealer.id+'-'+m.year+'-'+i).includes(r.id)||!Number.isInteger(r.years)||!saveFinite(r.years,dealer.used?2:0,dealer.used?13:0)||!saveFinite(r.condition,dealer.used?35:100,dealer.used?93:100)||!saveFinite(r.body,dealer.used?30:100,dealer.used?95:100)||!Object.hasOwn(carColors,r.paint)||!saveFinite(r.mileage,0,5000000)||typeof r.sold!=='boolean'||r.price!==(dealer.used?vehicleValue(model,r):model.price))return false;}}return true;}

function validateNewSystems(raw){const p=raw.pendingSideChoice;if(p!==undefined&&p!==null&&(!saveObject(p)||!raw.job||!raw.side||p.jobId!==raw.job||p.sideId!==raw.side.id||p.companyId!==raw.employment?.companyId))return false;
if(raw.admissionAttempts!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(raw.admissionAttempts)||raw.admissionAttempts.length>400||!raw.admissionAttempts.every(a=>saveObject(a)&&Number.isInteger(a.age)&&saveFinite(a.age,0,raw.age)&&['college','university'].includes(a.level)&&Object.hasOwn(a.level==='college'?colleges:universities,a.institution)&&Object.hasOwn(faculties,a.facultyId)&&saveFinite(a.score,0,100)&&typeof a.accepted==='boolean')))return false;if(raw.admissionPreparation!==undefined&&raw.admissionPreparation!==null&&(!saveObject(raw.admissionPreparation)||!Number.isInteger(raw.admissionPreparation.age)||!saveFinite(raw.admissionPreparation.age,0,raw.age)))return false;
for(const e of [raw.employment,...(Array.isArray(raw.jobMarket?.vacancies)?raw.jobMarket.vacancies:[]),...(Array.isArray(raw.recruiterOffers)?raw.recruiterOffers:[])])if(e){if(e.schedule!==undefined&&!Object.hasOwn(jobSchedules,e.schedule))return false;for(const k of ['hoursRequestYear','reducedUntil'])if(e[k]!==undefined&&!saveFinite(e[k],-1,raw.age+1))return false;if(e.hoursReduction!==undefined&&![4,8,12].includes(e.hoursReduction))return false;}
for(const c of raw.childrenRecords||[]){if(c.trust!==undefined&&!saveFinite(c.trust,0,100))return false;if(c.careHours!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(c.careHours)||!saveFinite(c.careHours,0,12)))return false;if(c.gifts!==undefined&&(!saveObject(c.gifts)||!saveFinite(c.gifts.normalYear,-1,raw.age)))return false;for(const k of ['talkYear','activityYear','supportYear'])if(c[k]!==undefined&&!saveFinite(c[k],-1,raw.age))return false;}
const validPractice=r=>saveObject(r)&&/^c[0-9]{1,12}$/.test(r.companyId)&&saveText(r.companyName,120)&&saveText(r.role,150)&&saveText(r.site,30)&&Object.hasOwn(jobTracks,r.jobId)&&saveSkillKeys.includes(r.sector)&&validWorkLocation(r.location)&&saveFinite(r.hours,0,12)&&saveFinite(r.startAge,0,raw.age)&&typeof r.creditedJob==='boolean'&&typeof r.success==='boolean';
if(raw.practiceHistory!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(raw.practiceHistory)||raw.practiceHistory.length>100||!raw.practiceHistory.every(r=>validPractice(r)&&saveFinite(r.completedAge,0,raw.age))))return false;for(const q of [raw.college,raw.university])if(q){if(q.practiceDone!==undefined&&typeof q.practiceDone!=='boolean')return false;if(q.practice!==undefined&&q.practice!==null&&!validPractice(q.practice))return false;}return true;}

function validateSocialFamilySave(raw){
 const gender=p=>p.gender===undefined||['male','female'].includes(p.gender),id=s=>typeof s==='string'&&/^\d{1,8}$/.test(s),networkId=s=>typeof s==='string'&&/^n\d{1,8}$/.test(s);
 if(raw.appVersion!==undefined&&(typeof raw.appVersion!=='string'||!/^\d{1,3}\.\d{1,3}\.\d{1,3}$/.test(raw.appVersion)))return false;
 if(raw.cityName!==undefined&&!saveText(raw.cityName,100))return false;
 if(raw.birthProfile!==undefined&&(!saveObject(raw.birthProfile)||!saveText(raw.birthProfile.countryName,100)||!Number.isInteger(raw.birthProfile.wealth)||!wealthLevels[raw.birthProfile.wealth]))return false;
 for(const p of [...(raw.parents||[]),...(raw.friends||[]),...(raw.childrenRecords||[])])if(!gender(p))return false;
 if(raw.relatives!==undefined){if(!Array.isArray(raw.relatives)||raw.relatives.length>12||new Set(raw.relatives.map(r=>r?.id)).size!==raw.relatives.length)return false;for(const r of raw.relatives)if(!saveObject(r)||typeof r.id!=='string'||!/^r\d{1,4}$/.test(r.id)||!kinRoles.includes(r.role)||!['family','maternal','paternal'].includes(r.branch)||!saveText(r.name,100)||!['male','female'].includes(r.gender)||!['alive','deceased'].includes(r.status)||!Number.isInteger(r.age)||!saveFinite(r.age,0,150)||!saveFinite(r.bond,0,100)||!saveFinite(r.trust,0,100)||!saveObject(r.gifts)||!saveFinite(r.gifts.normalYear,-1,raw.age)||!saveFinite(r.interactionYear,-1,raw.age))return false;}
 for(const f of raw.friends||[]){if(f.playerFeelings!==undefined&&!saveFinite(f.playerFeelings,0,100)||f.flirtYear!==undefined&&!saveFinite(f.flirtYear,-1,raw.age))return false;for(const k of ['hasBeenFriend','formerFriend'])if(f[k]!==undefined&&typeof f[k]!=='boolean')return false;if(f.networkIds!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(f.networkIds)||f.networkIds.length>3||new Set(f.networkIds).size!==f.networkIds.length||!f.networkIds.every(networkId)))return false;}
 for(const key of ['nextNetworkId','nextInvitationId'])if(raw[key]!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(raw[key])||!saveFinite(raw[key],1,1e8)))return false;
 if(raw.networkPeople!==undefined){if(!Array.isArray(raw.networkPeople)||raw.networkPeople.length>60||new Set(raw.networkPeople.map(p=>p?.id)).size!==raw.networkPeople.length)return false;for(const p of raw.networkPeople)if(!saveObject(p)||!networkId(p.id)||!saveText(p.name,100)||!['male','female'].includes(p.gender)||!Number.isInteger(p.age)||!saveFinite(p.age,0,150)||!['alive','deceased'].includes(p.status)||!['supportive','creative','reckless'].includes(p.kind)||p.metFriendId!==null&&!id(p.metFriendId))return false;}
 if(raw.invitations!==undefined){if(!Array.isArray(raw.invitations)||raw.invitations.length>40||new Set(raw.invitations.map(i=>i?.id)).size!==raw.invitations.length)return false;for(const i of raw.invitations){if(!saveObject(i)||typeof i.id!=='string'||!/^invite-\d{1,8}$/.test(i.id)||!Number.isInteger(i.age)||!saveFinite(i.age,0,raw.age)||!['parent','friend','partner'].includes(i.hostType)||!id(i.hostId)||!Object.hasOwn(invitationActivities,i.activity)||i.hostType==='parent'&&i.activity==='date'||!saveFinite(i.fee,0,10000)||![.25,.5].includes(i.resource)||!['pending','accepted','declined','expired'].includes(i.status)||!Array.isArray(i.guests)||i.guests.length>6)return false;for(const g of i.guests)if(!saveObject(g)||!['friend','network'].includes(g.type)||!(g.type==='friend'?id(g.id):networkId(g.id))||!saveText(g.name,100)||!['male','female'].includes(g.gender)||!saveFinite(g.age,0,150)||!saveText(g.relationship,120))return false;}}
 const pending=[raw.event,...(raw.pendingEvents||[])].filter(e=>e).flatMap(e=>e.choices.filter(c=>c.effect==='invitation'));
 for(const c of pending){const i=raw.invitations?.find(i=>i.id===c.invitationId&&i.status==='pending');if(!i)return false;if(c.meetId&&!i.guests.some(g=>g.type==='network'&&g.id===c.meetId))return false;}
 return true;
}
function validPersonRef(ref){if(!saveObject(ref)||typeof ref.id!=='string')return false;return ref.type==='parent'?/^[0-3]$/.test(ref.id):ref.type==='relative'?/^r\d{1,4}$/.test(ref.id):ref.type==='friend'?/^\d{1,8}$/.test(ref.id):ref.type==='colleague'?/^co\d+$/.test(ref.id):ref.type==='story'?/^a\d{1,12}$/.test(ref.id):false;}
function validateFriendshipAptitudes(raw){
 if(raw.bestFriendId!==undefined&&raw.bestFriendId!==null&&(typeof raw.bestFriendId!=='string'||!raw.friends?.some(f=>f.id===raw.bestFriendId)))return false;
 for(const f of raw.friends||[]){if(f.deepTrust!==undefined&&!saveFinite(f.deepTrust,0,100))return false;if(f.bestCount!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(f.bestCount)||!saveFinite(f.bestCount,0,120)))return false;for(const key of ['bestLostAge','bestAskAge'])if(f[key]!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(f[key])||!saveFinite(f[key],-1,raw.age)))return false;if(f.rebuildYears!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(f.rebuildYears)||f.rebuildYears.length>121||new Set(f.rebuildYears).size!==f.rebuildYears.length||!f.rebuildYears.every(y=>Number.isInteger(y)&&saveFinite(y,0,raw.age))))return false;
 if(f.deepProgress!==undefined){const p=f.deepProgress;if(!saveObject(p)||!Number.isInteger(p.age)||!saveFinite(p.age,0,raw.age)||!saveFinite(p.earned,0,12)||!Array.isArray(p.channels)||p.channels.length>5||new Set(p.channels).size!==p.channels.length||!p.channels.every(c=>['time','gift','help','event','best'].includes(c)))return false;}
 if(f.bestActivities!==undefined&&(!saveObject(f.bestActivities)||Object.keys(f.bestActivities).length!==3||!['bestTrip','bestProject','bestTalk'].every(key=>Number.isInteger(f.bestActivities[key])&&saveFinite(f.bestActivities[key],-1,raw.age))))return false;}
 if(raw.aptitudes!==undefined){const a=raw.aptitudes;if(!saveObject(a)||!saveObject(a.values)||Object.keys(a.values).length!==8||!Object.keys(aptitudeNames).every(key=>saveFinite(a.values[key],0,100))||!Object.hasOwn(aptitudeNames,a.focus)||!Number.isInteger(a.hintAge)||!saveFinite(a.hintAge,7,13)||typeof a.hintShown!=='boolean')return false;}
 const a=raw.school?.clubAdvice;if(a!==undefined){if(!saveObject(a)||!['new','pending','declined','accepted','closed'].includes(a.status)||!Number.isInteger(a.attempts)||!saveFinite(a.attempts,0,2))return false;for(const key of ['firstAge','offeredAge'])if(!Number.isInteger(a[key])||!saveFinite(a[key],-1,raw.age))return false;if(!Number.isInteger(a.nextAge)||!saveFinite(a.nextAge,-1,raw.age+1)||a.club!==undefined&&!['football','computer','science','debate','theatre','volunteer'].includes(a.club))return false;}
 if(raw.storyPeople!==undefined){if(!Array.isArray(raw.storyPeople)||raw.storyPeople.length>64||new Set(raw.storyPeople.map(p=>p?.id)).size!==raw.storyPeople.length)return false;for(const p of raw.storyPeople)if(!saveObject(p)||typeof p.id!=='string'||!/^a\d{1,12}$/.test(p.id)||!saveText(p.key,120)||!storyRoles.includes(p.role)||!saveText(p.name,100)||!['male','female'].includes(p.gender)||!Number.isInteger(p.age)||!saveFinite(p.age,0,150)||!['alive','retired','deceased'].includes(p.status)||p.friendId!==null&&(typeof p.friendId!=='string'||!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(p.friendId)))return false;}
 if(raw.employment?.colleagues!==undefined&&!Array.isArray(raw.employment.colleagues))return false;
 for(const p of raw.employment?.colleagues||[]){if(!saveObject(p))return false;if(p.friendId!==undefined&&(typeof p.friendId!=='string'||!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(p.friendId)))return false;if(p.gender!==undefined&&!['male','female'].includes(p.gender))return false;}
 for(const e of [raw.event,...(raw.pendingEvents||[])].filter(Boolean)){for(const ref of [e.personRef,...e.choices.map(c=>c.personRef)].filter(Boolean)){if(!validPersonRef(ref))return false;if(ref.type==='parent'&&!raw.parents?.[Number(ref.id)]||ref.type==='relative'&&!(Array.isArray(raw.relatives)&&raw.relatives.some(p=>p.id===ref.id))||ref.type==='friend'&&!raw.friends?.some(p=>p.id===ref.id)||ref.type==='colleague'&&!raw.employment?.colleagues?.some(p=>p.id===ref.id)||ref.type==='story'&&!raw.storyPeople?.some(p=>p.id===ref.id))return false;}}
 return true;
}

const degreeFields={general:'Общая программа',it:'Информационные технологии',business:'Экономика и управление',medicine:'Медицина · 6 лет',education:'Педагогика',arts:'Искусство',technical:'Инженерия',service:'Сервис',sports:'Физическая культура'};
const levels=['Начальная ступень','Специалист','Старший специалист','Ведущий специалист'];
const log=(s,text,kind='normal')=>{s.log.push({age:s.age,text,kind});refreshPersona(s);};
const cap=n=>Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(n)));
const professionalSkillNames={it:'IT',technical:'Техника',business:'Бизнес',education:'Образование',service:'Сервис',arts:'Искусство',sports:'Спорт',medicine:'Медицина'};
function initProgress(s){s.version=SAVE_VERSION;s.appVersion=APP_VERSION;initLife(s);initSport(s);s.pendingEvents??=[];s.recentEvents??=[];s.work??={level:0,raises:0,tenure:0,performance:50,overtime:0,requestYear:-1};s.xp??={};s.skills??={it:0,technical:0,business:0,education:0,service:0,arts:0,sports:0,medicine:0};s.pendingSideChoice??=null;s.side??=null;s.clubs??=[];s.friends??=[];s.nextFriendId??=1;for(const f of s.friends){f.links??={};f.status??='alive';}if(s.partner&&!s.partnerId){const f={id:String(s.nextFriendId++),name:s.partner,age:s.age,bond:s.relation,trust:Math.max(50,s.relation),kind:'supportive',links:{}};s.friends.push(f);s.partnerId=f.id;}s.creativeHours??=0;s.portfolio??=0;initCreative(s);s.legacyEducation??=Boolean(s.education&&!s.degreeField);s.degreeField??=s.education?'general':'it';s.yearEffort??=0;s.certificate??=false;s.scholarship??=false;initKinship(s);initGeography(s);initFamilyNames(s);initBusiness(s);initEstates(s);initStoryPeople(s);initFriendship(s);initAptitudes(s);initEducation(s);if(!schoolEnrolled(s))s.clubs=[];initPersona(s);initShopping(s);initAffection(s);cleanSocialLinks(s);initEmployment(s,careerJobs);initInternships(s);initLivingWorld(s);refreshPersona(s);return s;}
function grossSalary(s,econ,quote=false){const override=s.dev?.salaryOverride;if(s.job&&override&&override.jobId===s.job&&override.companyId===s.employment?.companyId)return Math.round(override.value*mobilityWorkFactor(s));const j=careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.job);if(!quote&&j&&workAccess(s,j.sector,s.employment?.companyId))return 0;return Math.round(mobilityWorkFactor(s)*(j?.salary||0)*econ.income*(econ.sectorDemand?.[j?.sector]||1)*(j?.field&&hasCollege(s,j.field)&&!hasUniversity(s,j.field)?.82:1)*(s.age<18?.75:1)*(enterpriseMultiplier(s)*(1+s.work.raises*.06)*workPayFactor(s)));}
function sideRate(s,econ,quote=false){const j=sideJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.side?.id);if(!quote&&j&&workAccess(s,j.sector,null,true))return 0;return j?Math.round(j.rate*econ.income*(1+Math.min(1,(s.xp[j.sector]||0)*.1))*100)/100:0;}
function weeklyHours(s){return Math.round((schoolHours(s)+(s.job&&!workAccess(s,careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.job)?.sector,s.employment?.companyId)?effectiveWorkHours(s)+s.work.overtime*medicalWorkFactor(s):0)+(s.side&&!workAccess(s,sideJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.side.id)?.sector,null,true)?s.side.hours*medicalWorkFactor(s):0)+s.clubs.reduce((n,id)=>n+(clubs[id]?.hours||0),0)+s.creativeHours+s.yearEffort+sportHours(s)+caregivingHours(s)+commute(s).hours+businessHours(s)+householdHours(s))*10)/10;}
function forecast(s,econ){const base=Math.round(grossSalary(s,econ)*medicalWorkFactor(s));const extra=Math.round(base/(workHours(s)||40)*s.work.overtime*1.25);const side=Math.round(medicalWorkFactor(s)*sideRate(s,econ)*(s.side?.hours||0)*(s.age<18?36:48));const art=Math.round(creativeIncome(s,econ)*medicalWorkFactor(s));const endorsements=sponsorshipIncome(s),business=businessProjection(s).dividend;const sport=Math.round(sportSalary(s,econ)*medicalWorkFactor(s));const retirement=pension(s,econ);const practice=internshipIncome(s,econ);const gross=base+extra+side+art+sport+practice+endorsements;const tax=Math.round(gross*(gross>40000?.18:gross>18000?.12:.04));return {base,extra,side,art,sport,practice,endorsements,business,gross,tax,net:gross-tax+retirement+business,retirement,hours:weeklyHours(s)};}
function jobRequirement(s,j,skipMigration=false){if(!skipMigration){const access=workAccess(s,j.sector);if(access)return access;}if(s.retired)return 'Сначала выбери возвращение к работе в настройках пенсии.';if(professionalSport(s))return 'Сначала заверши профессиональный спортивный контракт.';if(s.age<minimumJobAge(j.id))return 'С '+minimumJobAge(j.id)+' лет';if(j.id==='athlete'&&!s.sportsOffer)return 'Нужно приглашение команды';if(j.creative&&s.creativity<(hasCollege(s,'arts')?Math.min(j.creative,45):j.creative))return `Творчество от ${hasCollege(s,'arts')?45:j.creative}`;if(j.portfolio&&s.portfolio<j.portfolio)return `Портфолио от ${j.portfolio}`;if(j.certificate&&!s.certificate)return 'Нужны 2 года практики в мастерской';if(j.experience&&(s.xp.business||0)<j.experience)return '4 года опыта в бизнесе';if(s.iq<j.iq)return `Интеллект от ${j.iq}`;if(j.skill&&(s.skills[j.sector]||0)<j.skill)return `Навык «${professionalSkillNames[j.sector]}» от ${j.skill}`;if(j.edu&&!hasUniversity(s,j.field)&&!(hasCollege(s,j.field)&&!['doctor','director','analyst','engineer'].includes(j.id))){if(!(j.id==='developer'&&(s.xp.it||0)>=2&&s.skills.it>=60))return `Диплом: ${degreeFields[j.field]}`;}return null;}
function sideRequirement(s,j){const access=workAccess(s,j.sector,null,true);if(access)return access;if(s.age<j.age)return `С ${j.age} лет`;if(j.academics&&s.academics<j.academics)return `Учёба от ${j.academics}`;if(j.skill&&s.skills.it<j.skill)return `IT-навык от ${j.skill}`;if(j.creative&&s.creativity<(hasCollege(s,'arts')?Math.min(j.creative,45):j.creative))return `Творчество от ${hasCollege(s,'arts')?45:j.creative}`;return null;}
function available(s,cost=1){return !s.alive?'Жизнь завершена.':s.event?'Сначала реши событие.':s.prison?'Доступно после освобождения.':s.pendingSideChoice?'Сначала реши, оставлять ли подработку.':s.energy<cost?`Нужно ${cost} ресурса. На этот год недостаточно.`:null;}
function careerAction(s,id,value,econ,rng=Math.random){initProgress(s);if(id==='resolveSide'){if(!s.pendingSideChoice)return 'Выбор по подработке уже завершён.';if(!['keep','quit'].includes(value))return 'Выбери, оставить подработку или уйти.';if(value==='keep'&&weeklyHours(s)>80)return 'С обеими работами нагрузка выше 80 часов. Подработку нужно завершить.';if(value==='quit')s.side=null;s.pendingSideChoice=null;log(s,value==='quit'?'Ты завершил подработку и сосредоточился на основной работе.':'Ты сохранил подработку вместе с основной работой. Следи за отдыхом и нагрузкой.');return null;}if(['apply','acceptOffer','declineOffer','jobCourse','colleagueHelp','showResults','reduceHours'].includes(id))return employmentAction(s,id,value,econ,rng,{jobs:careerJobs,requirement:jobRequirement,hours:weeklyHours,available,salary:x=>grossSalary(x,econ)});const cost=careerCost(id),blocked=available(s,cost);if(blocked)return blocked;let text='';
if(['artHours','artLesson','artProject','artApply'].includes(id)){const result=creativeAction(s,id,value,econ,rng,weeklyHours);if(typeof result==='string')return result;s.energy-=cost;log(s,result.text);return null;}
switch(id){
case 'side':{const [key,hoursText]=String(value).split(':');const job=sideJobs.find(j=>j.id===key);const hours=Number(hoursText);if(!job||![4,8,12,16,20].includes(hours))return 'Выбери подработку и часы.';const requirement=sideRequirement(s,job);if(requirement)return requirement;if(s.age<18&&hours>16)return 'Во время школы — не более 16 часов подработки в неделю.';const before=s.side,oldHours=before?.hours||0;s.side={id:key,hours};if(hours>oldHours&&weeklyHours(s)>80){s.side=before;return 'Суммарная нагрузка превысит 80 часов в неделю.';}text=`Подработка: ${job.name}, ${hours} ч/нед. Ставка ${sideRate(s,econ)} $/ч. Оплата и последствия — в конце года.`;break;}
case 'stopSide':if(!s.side)return 'Подработки пока нет.';s.side=null;text='Подработка отменена на следующий расчётный год.';break;
case 'club':{const c=clubs[value];if(!Object.hasOwn(clubs,value)||!c||!schoolEnrolled(s)||s.age<c.age||s.age>=18)return 'Этот клуб доступен только школьникам подходящего возраста.';if(s.clubs.includes(value)){s.clubs=s.clubs.filter(x=>x!==value);text=`Ты покинул клуб «${c.name}».`;}else{if(s.clubs.length>=2)return 'Можно посещать не более двух клубов одновременно.';s.clubs.push(value);recordClubAcceptance(s,value);if(weeklyHours(s)>80){s.clubs.pop();return 'Для клуба не хватает свободного времени.';}text=`Теперь ты посещаешь «${c.name}»: ${c.hours} ч/нед.`;}break;}
case 'creativeHours':{const result=creativeAction(s,'artHours','drawing:'+value,econ,rng,weeklyHours);if(typeof result==='string')return result;text=result.text;break;}
case 'portfolio':{if(s.age<10)return 'Портфолио доступно с 10 лет.';const result=creativeAction(s,'artProject','drawing',econ,rng,weeklyHours);if(typeof result==='string')return result;text=result.text;break;}
case 'course':{if(s.age<12)return 'Дополнительные курсы доступны с 12 лет.';if(!['it','technical','business'].includes(value))return 'Выбери направление курса.';const fee=Math.round(150*econ.cost);if(s.money<fee)return `Нужно ${fee} $.`;s.money-=fee;s.skills[value]=cap(s.skills[value]+12);s.academics=cap(s.academics+2);text=`Курс «${professionalSkillNames[value]}» завершён: навык +12. Оплачено ${fee} $.`;break;}
case 'field':if(s.uni||s.education)return 'Направление уже начатого или завершённого образования изменить нельзя.';if(!Object.hasOwn(degreeFields,value)||value==='general')return 'Выбери профильное направление образования.';s.degreeField=value;log(s,`Выбрано направление: ${degreeFields[value]}.`);return null;
case 'train':if(weeklyHours(s)+2>80)return 'Для обучения не хватает свободного времени.';if(!s.job)return 'Сначала устройся на работу.';if(s.employment.trainedYear===s.age)return 'Рабочее обучение уже пройдено в этом году.';s.employment.trainedYear=s.age;s.skills[careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.job).sector]=cap(s.skills[careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.job).sector]+10);s.work.performance=cap(s.work.performance+6);s.yearEffort+=2;text='Ты прошёл рабочее обучение: навык +10, результативность +6, нагрузка +2 ч/нед до конца года.';break;
case 'overtime':if(!s.job)return 'Сначала устройся на работу.';{const hours=Number(value);if(![0,5,10].includes(hours))return 'Дополнительные часы: 0, 5 или 10.';if(s.age<18&&hours>0)return 'До 18 лет сверхурочные недоступны: действует сокращённый дневной график.';const old=s.work.overtime;s.work.overtime=hours;if(hours>old&&weeklyHours(s)>80){s.work.overtime=old;return 'Нагрузка превысит 80 часов в неделю.';}text=`Дополнительная работа: ${hours} ч/нед, оплата с коэффициентом 1,25.`;}break;
case 'promotion':case 'raise':{if(!s.job)return 'Сначала устройся на работу.';if(s.work.requestYear===s.age)return 'Запрос руководству можно отправить один раз в год.';s.work.requestYear=s.age;const job=careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.job);const skill=s.skills[job.sector]||0;const readiness=promotionReadiness(s,careerJobs);const ready=id==='raise'?s.work.tenure>=1&&s.work.performance>=70&&skill>=35:readiness.ready;if(!ready)text='Руководство отказало. '+readiness.reason;else if(rng()>Math.max(.6,Math.min(.8,.7+(s.personality.discipline-50)/500)))text='Результаты оценили, но бюджета на повышение в этом году нет.';else if(id==='promotion'){if(s.work.level>=3)text='На текущей должности достигнута верхняя ступень. Можно искать новую роль.';else{s.work.level++;s.work.stageYear=s.work.tenure;s.work.performance-=8;text=`Повышение одобрено: ${jobTitle(s)} в «${s.employment.companyName}». Базовая зарплата выросла.`;}}else{if(s.work.raises>=5)text='Достигнут потолок индивидуальных прибавок. Следующий шаг — новая должность.';else{s.work.raises++;text='Прибавка одобрена: +6% от базовой ставки.';}}break;}
case 'quit':if(!s.job)return 'У тебя нет постоянной работы.';s.job=null;s.employment=null;s.work={level:0,raises:0,tenure:0,performance:50,overtime:0,requestYear:-1};text='Ты уволился. Опыт и навыки сохранены.';break;
default:return 'Неизвестное карьерное действие.';
}shapePersonality(s,id);s.energy-=cost;log(s,text);return null;}
function progressYear(s,econ,rng=Math.random){initProgress(s);if(s.prison)return {net:0,gross:0};const pay=forecast(s,econ);const actualBusiness=settleBusinessYear(s,rng,pay.hours);pay.net+=actualBusiness.dividend-pay.business;pay.business=actualBusiness.dividend;internshipYear(s,pay.hours);const trainedSport=!!activeSport(s)&&s.sport.hours>0&&!s.sport.injury&&!medicalRestriction(s);sportYear(s,econ,rng);if(trainedSport&&pay.hours<=80)s.skills.sports=cap(s.skills.sports+aptitudeGain(s,'sports',Math.max(1,Math.min(5,Math.floor(s.sport.hours/4)))));sponsorshipYear(s);const load=pay.hours;const excess=Math.max(0,load-48);if(excess){const damage=Math.ceil(excess/5);s.health=cap(s.health-damage);s.happy=cap(s.happy-Math.ceil(excess/4));if(s.age<18||s.uni||s.college)s.academics=cap(s.academics-Math.ceil(excess/2));log(s,`Нагрузка ${load} ч/нед: здоровье −${damage}, настроение −${Math.ceil(excess/4)}${s.age<18||s.uni||s.college?`, учёба −${Math.ceil(excess/2)}`:''}.`);}else if(s.age>=6&&s.age<18||s.uni||s.college)s.academics=cap(s.academics+3);
if(s.job&&pay.base>0){const j=careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.job);if(j){s.pensionYears++;s.pensionEarnings+=pay.base;s.xp[j.sector]=(s.xp[j.sector]||0)+1;s.skills[j.sector]=cap(s.skills[j.sector]+aptitudeGain(s,j.sector,7));s.work.tenure++;s.work.performance=cap(s.work.performance+6+Math.floor((s.personality.discipline-50)/25)+(s.skills[j.sector]>=50?3:0)-(load>60?12:0));s.work.warnings=s.work.performance<25?(s.work.warnings||0)+1:0;if(s.work.warnings===1)log(s,'Руководство предупредило о низких результатах. Нужно снизить перегрузку и улучшить навыки.');if(s.work.warnings>=2||rng()<.012){const reason=s.work.warnings>=2?'после повторного предупреждения':'из-за сокращения должности';log(s,`Работа потеряна ${reason}. Опыт, навыки и пенсионный стаж сохранены.`,'chapter');s.job=null;s.work={level:0,raises:0,tenure:0,performance:50,overtime:0,requestYear:-1};}}}
if(pay.base>0)enterpriseYear(s,careerJobs,econ,rng,x=>grossSalary(x,econ));
if(s.side&&pay.side>0){const job=sideJobs.find(j=>j.id===s.side.id);if(job){const progress=s.side.hours/12;s.xp[job.sector]=(s.xp[job.sector]||0)+progress;s.skills[job.sector]=cap(s.skills[job.sector]+aptitudeGain(s,job.sector,Math.max(2,s.side.hours)));if(job.physical)s.health=cap(s.health-Math.ceil(s.side.hours/8));if(job.sector==='technical'&&s.xp.technical>=2&&!s.certificate){s.certificate=true;log(s,'Практика в мастерской пройдена. Теперь доступна профессия электрика.','chapter');}}}
for(const id of s.clubs){if(id==='football'){const record=ensureSportRecord(s,'football');record.skill=cap(record.skill+aptitudeGain(s,'sports',8));record.reputation=cap(record.reputation+2);if(record.skill>=20&&record.tier===0)record.tier=1;s.sports=cap(s.sports+aptitudeGain(s,'sports',12));s.skills.sports=cap(s.skills.sports+aptitudeGain(s,'sports',8));}if(id==='computer')s.skills.it=cap(s.skills.it+aptitudeGain(s,'it',12));if(id==='science'){s.academics=cap(s.academics+8);s.iq=cap(s.iq+2);}if(id==='debate')s.skills.business=cap(s.skills.business+aptitudeGain(s,'business',8));if(id==='theatre'){s.skills.arts=cap(s.skills.arts+aptitudeGain(s,'arts',8));s.creativity=cap(s.creativity+aptitudeGain(s,'arts',10));s.portfolio=cap(s.portfolio+4);creativeStudy(s,'acting',8,4);}if(id==='volunteer'){s.happy=cap(s.happy+4);s.skills.service=cap(s.skills.service+aptitudeGain(s,'service',6));}}
creativeYear(s,load);
if(pay.gross||pay.business)log(s,`Заработано: ${pay.gross} $ до налогов; налоги ${pay.tax} $; на руки ${pay.net} $. Подработка ${pay.side} $, дополнительные часы ${pay.extra} $, творчество ${pay.art} $, спорт ${pay.sport} $, рекламные контракты ${pay.endorsements} $, выплаты бизнеса ${pay.business} $.`,'finance');
if(s.age===17&&s.clubs.length){s.clubs=[];log(s,'Школьные клубы завершены. Полученные навыки сохранятся.','chapter');}s.yearEffort=0;return pay;}
function socialAction(s,id,target,econ,rng=Math.random){initProgress(s);if(['bestAsk','bestTrip','bestProject','bestTalk'].includes(id))return bestFriendAction(s,id,target,econ,rng);const cost=socialCost(id),blocked=available(s,cost);if(blocked)return blocked;if(id.startsWith('relative:'))return relativeAction(s,id,String(target));if(s.age<5&&!['parent:talk','parent:help','parent:argue'].includes(id))return 'Самостоятельные знакомства доступны с 5 лет.';let text='';
if(id==='meet'){if(s.friends.filter(f=>f.bond>0).length>=12)return 'У тебя уже большой круг общения: удели время существующим друзьям.';if(rng()>Math.max(.75,Math.min(.95,.85+(s.personality.sociability-50)/500)))text='Сегодня знакомство не завязалось. Можно попробовать позже.';else{const friend=spawnFriend(s,rng,false);text=`Новое знакомство: ${friend.name}. Чтобы подружиться, нужно общаться.`;}}
else if(id.startsWith('parent:')){const p=s.parents[Number(target)];if(!p||p.status==='deceased')return 'С этим родителем нельзя поговорить.';const action=id.split(':')[1];if(action==='talk'){p.bond=cap(p.bond+10);s.happy=cap(s.happy+4);text=s.age<3?`Ты провёл время с ${p.name}: голос, улыбки и внимание укрепляют привязанность.`:`Ты поговорил с ${p.name}. Стало легче понимать друг друга.`;}else if(action==='argue'){if(s.age<5)return 'Пока лучше побыть рядом с родителем.';p.bond=cap(p.bond-15);s.happy=cap(s.happy-3);text=`Спор с ${p.name} ухудшил отношения.`;}else if(action==='help'){if(s.age<3)return 'Помогать с делами можно с 3 лет.';p.bond=cap(p.bond+14);s.happy=cap(s.happy+3);text=`Ты помог родителю ${p.name} с делами.`;}else return 'Неизвестное действие.';}
else{const f=s.friends.find(f=>f.id===String(target));if(!f||(f.bond<=0&&id!=='breakup'))return 'Этот человек сейчас вне твоего круга общения.';switch(id){case 'hangout':if(rng()>.92){f.bond=cap(f.bond-2);text=`${f.name} не смог(ла) встретиться: сейчас другие планы. Отношения немного охладели.`;break;}sharedFeelings(s,f,6);deepenTrust(s,f,'time',3);f.bond=cap(f.bond+12);f.trust=cap(f.trust+7);s.happy=cap(s.happy+5);text=(f.country&&f.country!==s.country||s.mobility?.assignment?.days>=180)?`Разговор по видеосвязи с ${f.name} укрепил дружбу.`:`Время с ${f.name} укрепило дружбу.`;break;case 'support':sharedFeelings(s,f,4);deepenTrust(s,f,'help',3);f.trust=cap(f.trust+14+(s.personality.empathy>=65?2:0));f.bond=cap(f.bond+6);text=`Ты поддержал ${f.name} в сложный момент. Доверие выросло.`;break;case 'argue':sharedFeelings(s,f,-10);f.bond=cap(f.bond-18);f.trust=cap(f.trust-10);text=`Ссора с ${f.name} оставила неприятный осадок.`;break;case 'apologize':if(rng()<f.trust/100+.25){f.bond=cap(f.bond+9);f.trust=cap(f.trust+4);text=`${f.name} принял(а) извинения.`;}else text='Сейчас человек не готов к примирению.';break;case 'distance':f.bond=cap(f.bond-25);text=`Ты решил меньше общаться с ${f.name}.`;break;case 'flirt':if(!canRomance(s,f))return 'Проявлять романтическую симпатию можно к человеку другого пола с 16 лет.';if(f.flirtYear===s.age)return 'В этом году ты уже проявлял симпатию. Дай человеку время.';if(f.romanticBoundary)return 'Человек просит сохранить дружбу.';f.flirtYear=s.age;f.playerFeelings=cap(f.playerFeelings+12);if(f.romanticInterest&&rng()<Math.min(.9,.35+f.compatibility/150)){f.feelings=cap(f.feelings+8);text=`${f.name} тепло отреагировал(а) на твоё внимание. Симпатия может быть взаимной.`;}else text=`${f.name} пока не ответил(а) взаимностью. Твои чувства и чувства другого человека могут различаться.`;break;case 'romance':if(s.gender===f.gender)return 'Романтические отношения доступны только между разными полами.';if(s.age<16||f.age<16)return 'Отношения доступны с 16 лет и с ровесниками от 16.';if(s.partner)return 'Сначала нужно завершить текущие отношения.';if(f.bond<65||f.trust<55)return 'Пока не хватает близости 65 и доверия 55.';if(f.romanceYear===s.age)return 'В этом году чувства уже обсуждались. Дай человеку время.';
if(!f.romanticInterest||f.romanticBoundary){f.romanceYear=s.age;f.romanticBoundary=true;f.feelings=0;text=`${f.name} ценит дружбу, но не испытывает романтического интереса. Подарки и настойчивость не изменят этот выбор.`;}
else{if(f.feelings<45||f.playerFeelings<45)return `Для отношений нужна симпатия от 45 в обе стороны. Твоя: ${f.playerFeelings}, к тебе: ${f.feelings}.`;f.romanceYear=s.age;if(rng()<romanceChance(s,f)){s.partner=f.name;s.partnerId=f.id;s.relation=f.bond;f.feelings=Math.max(f.feelings,60);f.playerFeelings=Math.max(f.playerFeelings,60);text=`Вы с ${f.name} решили стать парой.`;}else{f.bond=cap(f.bond-3);f.feelings=cap(f.feelings-4);text='Человек пока не готов к отношениям. Взаимность нельзя гарантировать.';}}break;case 'breakup':if(s.partnerId!==f.id)return 'Вы не состоите в отношениях.';s.partner=null;s.partnerId=null;s.married=false;s.relation=0;f.bond=cap(f.bond-20);f.feelings=cap(f.feelings-25);text=`Вы с ${f.name} расстались.`;break;default:return 'Неизвестное действие.';}
if(s.partnerId===f.id)s.relation=f.bond;}
cleanSocialLinks(s);shapePersonality(s,id);s.energy-=cost;log(s,text);return null;}
function socialYear(s,rng=Math.random){pruneFriends(s);for(const f of s.friends){if(f.status==='deceased')continue;f.age++;if(f.age>=110||(f.age>70&&rng()<.005+(f.age-70)*.003)){f.status='deceased';f.bond=0;s.happy=cap(s.happy-10);s.stress=cap(s.stress+8);log(s,`Умер близкий человек: ${f.name}. Возраст — ${ageText(f.age)}.`,'chapter');if(s.partnerId===f.id){s.partner=null;s.partnerId=null;s.married=false;s.relation=0;}continue;}if(f.bond<=0)continue;if(rng()>.97&&s.partnerId!==f.id){f.bond=cap(f.bond-8);f.trust=cap(f.trust-3);log(s,`${f.name} решил(а) реже общаться: интересы и планы расходятся.`,'friend');}f.feelings=cap(f.feelings-(s.partnerId===f.id?1:2));f.playerFeelings=cap(f.playerFeelings-(s.partnerId===f.id?1:2));f.bond=cap(f.bond-4);if(f.kind==='reckless'&&f.bond>=60){s.crimeAffinity=cap(s.crimeAffinity+3);for(const p of s.parents)if(p.status!=='deceased')p.bond=cap(p.bond-2);}if(f.kind==='supportive'&&f.bond>=60)s.happy=cap(s.happy+2);if(f.kind==='creative'&&f.bond>=60)s.creativity=cap(s.creativity+2);if(s.partnerId===f.id)s.relation=f.bond;}
for(const p of s.parents)if(p.status!=='deceased')p.bond=cap(p.bond-2);if(s.partner&&!s.partnerId)s.relation=cap(s.relation-5);if(s.partner&&s.relation<20){log(s,`${s.partner} решил(а) расстаться: отношения остыли.`,'chapter');s.partner=null;s.partnerId=null;s.married=false;}for(const p of s.networkPeople)if(!p.metFriendId&&p.status!=='deceased')p.age++;cleanSocialLinks(s);}
function contextEvents(s){const opt=(label,result,changes)=>({label,result,changes,cost:0,effect:'regional'});const list=[];const circle=s.friends.filter(f=>f.bond>0);if(circle.length>=2){const [a,b]=circle;list.push({title:'Друзья поссорились',text:`${a.name} и ${b.name} спорят и просят тебя вмешаться.`,choices:[{...opt('Помочь им спокойно поговорить','Разговор помог друзьям лучше понять друг друга.',{happy:3}),friendLink:{a:a.id,b:b.id,delta:15}},{...opt('Поддержать только первого друга','Один друг оценил поддержку, другой почувствовал себя лишним.',{happy:-2}),friendId:a.id,bond:6,otherFriendId:b.id,otherBond:-8,friendLink:{a:a.id,b:b.id,delta:-10}}]});}if(s.age>=12&&(schoolEnrolled(s)||s.college||s.uni)&&circle.length)list.push({title:'Другие планы',text:'Друзья зовут гулять накануне важного учебного дня.',choices:[opt('Остаться готовиться','Подготовка помогла разобраться в материале.',{academics:5,happy:-2}),opt('Встретиться ненадолго','Прогулка подняла настроение, но времени на учёбу стало меньше.',{happy:6,academics:-3})]});if(s.job)list.push({phase:'work',title:'Непростая задача на работе',text:'Коллегам нужна помощь с задачей за пределами привычных обязанностей.',choices:[{...opt('Помочь команде','Ты приобрёл полезный опыт.',{happy:3}),performance:5},opt('Защитить время отдыха','Отдых помог восстановиться.',{health:4})]});if(s.side)list.push({phase:'side',title:'Сложный клиент',text:'Заказчик просит бесплатно переделать уже согласованную работу.',choices:[opt('Спокойно обсудить условия','Договорённость помогла сохранить силы.',{happy:3}),opt('Сделать всё заново','Клиент доволен, но ты устал.',{health:-3,happy:-2})]});const close=s.friends.find(f=>f.bond>=40);if(close)list.push({title:'Друг просит выслушать',text:'Близкому человеку нужен разговор после неудачного дня.',choices:[{...opt('Найти время','Разговор оказался важным для вас обоих.',{happy:4}),friendId:close.id,bond:8,trust:6},{...opt('Предложить поговорить завтра','Ты честно объяснил, что сегодня устал.',{health:2}),friendId:close.id,bond:-2}]});return list;}

function spawnFriend(s,rng=Math.random,announce=true,person=null){initProgress(s);pruneFriends(s);const gender=person?.gender||(rng()<.5?'male':'female'),opposite=gender!==s.gender,adult=s.age>=16&&(person?.age??s.age)>=16;const friend={id:String(s.nextFriendId++),status:'alive',gender,country:person?.country||s.country,citizenship:person?.citizenship||person?.country||s.country,name:person?.name||randomName(s.country,gender,rng),age:person?.age??Math.max(5,s.age+Math.floor(rng()*3)-1),bond:45,trust:40,kind:person?.kind||['supportive','creative','reckless'][Math.min(2,Math.floor(rng()*3))],links:{},romanticInterest:opposite&&rng()<.65,compatibility:30+Math.floor(rng()*61),feelings:0,playerFeelings:0,romanticBoundary:false,romanceYear:-1,flirtYear:-1,gifts:{normalYear:-1,romanticYear:-1},hasBeenFriend:false,formerFriend:false};if(opposite&&adult){friend.feelings=friend.romanticInterest?(rng()<.12?65+Math.floor(rng()*26):5+Math.floor(rng()*21)):0;friend.playerFeelings=rng()<.12?65+Math.floor(rng()*26):5+Math.floor(rng()*21);}for(const other of s.friends)if(other.status!=='deceased'&&other.bond>0&&!other.formerFriend){const bond=20+Math.floor(rng()*61);friend.links[other.id]=bond;other.links[friend.id]=bond;}s.friends.push(friend);initPersonLife(s,friend,'friend');if(announce)log(s,`Случайное знакомство: ${friend.name}. Контакт появился в отношениях.`,'friend');return friend;}

function pruneFriends(s){const protectedIds=new Set([s.partnerId,s.bestFriendId,...[s.event,...(s.pendingEvents||[])].filter(Boolean).flatMap(e=>[e.personRef?.type==='friend'?e.personRef.id:null,...e.choices.flatMap(c=>[c.friendId,c.otherFriendId,c.friendLink?.a,c.friendLink?.b,c.personRef?.type==='friend'?c.personRef.id:null])])]);const archived=s.friends.filter(f=>f.bond<=0&&!protectedIds.has(f.id));const expired=new Set(archived.slice(0,Math.max(0,archived.length-24)).map(f=>f.id));if(!expired.size)return;s.friends=s.friends.filter(f=>!expired.has(f.id));for(const f of s.friends)for(const id of Object.keys(f.links||{}))if(expired.has(id))delete f.links[id];}

// Stages overlap: a student can also work, train, create and run a business.
function activeLifeStages(s){return {
 childhood:s.age<9&&!s.school?.started,
 school:schoolEnrolled(s),
 college:!!s.college,
 university:s.uni>0,
 side:!!s.side,
 work:!!s.job,
 sport:!!s.sport?.active&&!s.sport.records[s.sport.active]?.retired,
 creative:s.creativeHours>0,
 business:!!s.business,
 adult:s.age>=18&&s.alive,
};}
const schoolEventIds=new Set(['bully','sciencefair','examfear','hackathon','peerparty','mentor','scholarshipcontest','careerday','summerstudy','counselor','newstudent']);
function eventStage(e){if(e.phase)return e.phase;if(schoolEventIds.has(e.id)||['Трудная контрольная','Новая компания','Выбор кружка','Выбор школьного кружка'].includes(e.title))return 'school';if(e.title==='Непростая задача на работе')return 'work';if(e.title==='Сложный клиент')return 'side';if(['firstword','sleep','sharing'].includes(e.id)||['Первые открытия','Семейный вечер'].includes(e.title))return 'childhood';if(['campus','resit','internshipfair'].includes(e.id))return 'student';return {worker:'work',side:'side',earner:'earner',sport:'sport',creative:'creative'}[e.tag]||null;}
function eventEligible(s,e){if(!e||!s.alive)return false;const stages=activeLifeStages(s),phase=eventStage(e);if(phase==='student')return stages.college||stages.university;if(phase==='earner')return stages.work||stages.side;if(phase&& !stages[phase])return false;
 // Queued decisions from older saves also need their original context.
 if(e.choices?.some(c=>c.effect==='business')&&!stages.business)return false;
 if(e.choices?.some(c=>c.effect==='assignment')&&!stages.work)return false;
 if(e.title==='Другие планы'&&!stages.school&&!stages.college&&!stages.university)return false;
 return true;}
function pruneEventQueue(s){const valid=[s.event,...(s.pendingEvents||[])].filter(e=>eventEligible(s,e));s.event=valid.shift()||null;s.pendingEvents=valid;}
