 ...s.parents.map((p,i)=>({key:'parent:'+i,role:p.role,person:p})),...s.relatives.map(p=>({key:'relative:'+p.id,role:kinRoleNames[p.role],person:p})),...s.childrenRecords.map(p=>({key:'child:'+p.id,role:'Ребёнок',person:p})),...s.friends.map(p=>({key:'friend:'+p.id,role:p.id===s.partnerId?s.married?'Супруг/супруга':'Партнёр':p.id===s.bestFriendId?'Лучший друг':'Друг / знакомый',person:p})),...(s.employment?.colleagues||[]).filter(p=>!p.friendId).map(p=>({key:'colleague:'+p.id,role:'Коллега',person:p})),...s.storyPeople.filter(p=>!p.friendId).map(p=>({key:'story:'+p.id,role:({teacher:'Преподаватель',doctor:'Врач',neighbor:'Сосед',peer:'Знакомый',manager:'Руководитель',client:'Клиент',coach:'Тренер',scout:'Скаут',author:'Автор',organizer:'Организатор',judge:'Судья',guardian:'Опекун',librarian:'Библиотекарь',mentor:'Наставник',stranger:'Знакомый',employee:'Сотрудник'})[p.role]||'Знакомый',person:p})),...s.networkPeople.filter(p=>!p.metFriendId).map(p=>({key:'network:'+p.id,role:'Знакомый друзей',person:p}))];}
const devNumber=(v,min,max,integer=false)=>typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=min&&v<=max&&(!integer||Number.isInteger(v));
function retimeDeveloper(s,age){const actors=new WeakSet(developerPeople(s).map(p=>p.person));for(const i of s.invitations||[])for(const g of i.guests)actors.add(g);const maps=new Set(['courses','visits','tasks','bestActivities','courseYears']);const special=new Set(['boughtAge','startedAge','phaseYear','approvedAge','lastDrawAge','lastPaymentAge','permanentSince','since','lastCountAge','prepYear','prAttemptYear','citizenAttemptYear','year','posted','completedAge','firstAge','offeredAge','acceptedAge','graduatedAge','lastSettledAge','joinedAge']);
 const walk=(o,parent='')=>{if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return;for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(o)){if(typeof v==='object'){if(k==='rebuildYears'){o[k]=[...new Set(v.map(n=>Math.min(n,age)))];continue;}if(k==='courseSupport'&&v?.paid)v.age=Math.min(v.age,age);walk(v,k);continue;}if(typeof v!=='number'||v<0)continue;if(k==='age'&&!actors.has(o)||/Year$/.test(k)||/Age$/.test(k)&&!['hintAge','startAge','nextAge'].includes(k)||special.has(k)||maps.has(parent)){o[k]=Math.min(v,age);}if(k==='nextAge')o[k]=Math.min(v,age+1);if(k==='startAge'&&o!==s.school)o[k]=Math.min(v,age);}};
 walk(s);s.age=age;s.event=null;s.pendingEvents=[];s.pendingSideChoice=null;s.jobMarket={age:-1,vacancies:[],applications:[]};s.recruiterOffers=[];s.admissionPreparation=null;s.mobility.workOffers=[];s.mobility.studyOffers=[];s.mobility.assignment=null;s.mobility.assignmentOffer=null;s.mobility.offerYear=-1;s.mobility.assignmentYear=-1;s.mobility.courseSupport={age,paid:s.parents.map(()=>0)};s.mobility.residence.lastCountAge=age;s.mobility.arrivalCourse&&(s.mobility.arrivalCourse.lastYear=Math.min(s.mobility.arrivalCourse.lastYear,age));for(const p of s.parents)if(p.educationSupport)p.educationSupport.age=Math.min(p.educationSupport.age,age);
}
function developerEdit(original,action,value={}){const s=structuredClone(original);initProgress(s);s.dev??={freeActions:false,salaryOverride:null};try{
 if(action==='character'){if(!devNumber(value.age,0,120,true)||!devNumber(value.money,-1e12,1e12))return {error:'Возраст: целое число от 0 до 120. Деньги: конечное число от −1 трлн до 1 трлн долларов.'};for(const key of Object.keys(devStats))if(!devNumber(value[key],0,100,true))return {error:'Все характеристики должны быть целыми числами от 0 до 100.'};if(value.age!==s.age)retimeDeveloper(s,value.age);s.money=value.money;for(const key of Object.keys(devStats))s[key]=value[key];}
 else if(action==='skills'){for(const [group,target,labels] of [['skills',s.skills,aptitudeNames],['aptitudes',s.aptitudes.values,aptitudeNames],['personality',s.personality,personalityTraits]])for(const key of Object.keys(labels)){const n=value[group]?.[key];if(!devNumber(n,0,100,true))return {error:'Навыки, склонности и характер: от 0 до 100.'};target[key]=n;}s.aptitudes.focus=Object.keys(s.aptitudes.values).reduce((a,b)=>s.aptitudes.values[a]>=s.aptitudes.values[b]?a:b);}
 else if(action==='job'){const j=careerJobs.find(j=>j.id===value.jobId);if(value.jobId!=='none'&&!j)return {error:'Выбери работу из списка.'};if(!devNumber(value.salary,0,1e9)||!devNumber(value.level,0,3,true))return {error:'Оклад: от 0 до 1 млрд $ в год. Ступень: от 0 до 3.'};s.job=j?.id||null;s.pendingSideChoice=null;s.work={level:value.level,raises:0,tenure:0,stageYear:0,performance:75,overtime:0,requestYear:-1};s.employment=null;initEmployment(s,careerJobs);if(j){s.employment.ladder=true;if(value.company){if(typeof value.company!=='string'||value.company.trim().length>120)return {error:'Название компании — до 120 символов.'};s.employment.companyName=value.company.trim();}s.dev.salaryOverride=value.standard?null:{jobId:j.id,companyId:s.employment.companyId,value:value.salary};}else s.dev.salaryOverride=null;}
 else if(action==='person'){const entry=developerPeople(s).find(p=>p.key===value.key);if(!entry)return {error:'Человек не найден.'};let p=entry.person;for(const key of ['bond','trust','feelings','playerFeelings'])if(!devNumber(value[key],0,100,true))return {error:'Отношения, доверие и чувства: от 0 до 100.'};p.bond=value.bond;p.trust=value.trust;if(!['keep','friend','best','partner','married','breakup'].includes(value.relation))return {error:'Выбери статус отношений.'};const family=['parent','relative','child'].includes(value.key.split(':')[0]);if(family&&value.relation!=='keep')return {error:'Для родственников меняются близость и доверие; их семейная роль сохраняется.'};if(value.relation!=='keep'&&!value.key.startsWith('friend:')){if(s.friends.length>=500)return {error:'Список друзей заполнен.'};const f={id:String(s.nextFriendId++),name:p.name,gender:p.gender||'male',age:p.age,bond:p.bond,trust:p.trust,kind:['supportive','creative','reckless'].includes(p.kind)?p.kind:'supportive',status:p.status==='deceased'?'deceased':'alive',country:p.country||s.country,citizenship:p.citizenship||p.country||s.country,links:{}};p.friendId=f.id;if(value.key.startsWith('network:'))p.metFriendId=f.id;s.friends.push(f);p=f;initAffection(s);initFriendship(s);}
 if(!family){const opposite=p.gender!==s.gender;p.feelings=opposite?value.feelings:0;p.playerFeelings=opposite?value.playerFeelings:0;if(value.relation!=='keep'){if(p.status==='deceased')return {error:'Нельзя назначить статус отношений умершему человеку.'};p.formerFriend=false;p.hasBeenFriend=true;if(value.relation==='best'){if(s.age<5||p.age<5)return {error:'Лучшая дружба доступна с 5 лет.'};s.bestFriendId=p.id;p.bond=Math.max(90,p.bond);p.deepTrust=100;p.bestCount=Math.max(1,p.bestCount||0);}if(['partner','married'].includes(value.relation)){if(s.age<16||p.age<16||!opposite)return {error:'Для пары нужны разные полы и возраст от 16 у обоих.'};s.partnerId=p.id;s.partner=p.name;s.married=value.relation==='married';s.relation=p.bond;p.romanticInterest=true;p.romanticBoundary=false;}if(value.relation==='breakup'||value.relation==='friend'){if(s.partnerId===p.id){s.partnerId=null;s.partner=null;s.married=false;s.relation=0;}if(s.bestFriendId===p.id)s.bestFriendId=null;}}if(s.partnerId===p.id)s.relation=p.bond;}}
 else if(action==='energy'){s.energy=4;}
 else if(action==='heal'){s.health=100;s.happy=100;s.stress=0;}
 else if(action==='school'){s.school.started=true;s.school.completed9=true;s.school.completed11=true;s.school.grade=11;s.school.route='finished';s.school.clubAdvice.status='closed';s.clubs=[];}
 else if(action==='licence'){s.city.licence={theory:100,practice:100,passed:true,examYear:s.age};}
 else if(action==='clearEvents'){s.event=null;s.pendingEvents=[];s.pendingSideChoice=null;}
 else if(action==='release'){s.prison=0;s.wanted=0;}
 else if(action==='revive'){s.alive=true;s.health=100;s.happy=80;s.stress=0;s.prison=0;s.event=null;s.pendingEvents=[];}
 else if(action==='debt'){if(s.money<0)s.money=0;for(const l of s.studyFinance.loans){l.principal=0;l.status='paid';}if(s.business)s.business.loans=[];}
 else if(action==='freeActions'){if(typeof value.enabled!=='boolean')return {error:'Неверный переключатель.'};s.dev.freeActions=value.enabled;if(value.enabled)s.energy=4;}
 else return {error:'Неизвестное действие.'};
 if(s.health===0&&s.alive)endLife(s,'Здоровье достигло нуля.');refreshPersona(s);if(!validateSave(s))return {error:'Изменения нарушают совместимость сохранения. Исходная жизнь сохранена.'};return {state:s};
 }catch{return {error:'Не удалось применить параметры. Исходная жизнь сохранена.'};}}

const actionArrow='<svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 16 16 4M5 4h11v11" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
function actionButton(id){const a={...actions[id]};
if(id==='therapy')a.hint=`${state.age<18?'Оплачивают взрослые':money(medicalQuote(state,profile(state),'consult'))} · осмотр и диагностика`;
if(id==='university')a.hint=`От 18 лет · ${money(tuition({...state,scholarship:state.academics>=40&&state.iq>=70}))}/год · 4 года`;
if(id==='child')a.hint=`Партнёр · от 21 года · ${money(4500*profile(state).cost)}/год`;
if(id==='house')a.hint=`${money(housePrice(profile(state)))} · остаются расходы на содержание`;if(id==='rent')a.hint=`От 18 лет · ${money(9000*profile(state).cost)}/год`;
return `<button type="button" class="action" data-action="${id}" ${!state.alive||state.prison||state.event||state.energy<actionCost(id)||state.age<({study:6,exercise:5,creative:5,peers:12,schoolJob:14,tutor:16,freelance:16}[id]||0)?'disabled':''}><span class="action-icon">${a.icon==='↗'?actionArrow:a.icon}</span><span><b>${a.title}</b><small>${a.hint}</small></span><span class="arrow" aria-hidden="true">${actionArrow}</span></button>`;}
function showNotification(){if(state.pendingSideChoice){const d=$('#employmentDecision');$('#sideChoiceText').textContent='Основная работа принята. Можно сохранить подработку, если хватает времени, или завершить её. Суммарная нагрузка '+forecast(state,profile(state)).hours+' ч/нед.';$('#employmentDecision [data-side-choice=keep]').disabled=forecast(state,profile(state)).hours>80;if($('#notification').open)$('#notification').close();if(!d.open){$('#sideChoiceError').textContent='';d.showModal();}return;}if($('#employmentDecision').open)$('#employmentDecision').close();const dialog=$('#notification');const item=queue[0];if(!item&&!state.event){if(dialog.open)dialog.close();$('#notificationChoices').replaceChildren();return;}
$('#notificationError').textContent='';const decision=!item;$('#notificationTag').textContent=decision?'МОМЕНТ ВЫБОРА':`${ageText(state.age).toUpperCase()} · СОБЫТИЕ`;
$('#notificationTitle').textContent=decision?state.event.title:item.title||(item.kind==='finance'?'Итоги года':item.kind==='chapter'?'Новая глава':'В твоей жизни');
if(!decision&&item.birthSummary){$('#notificationText').innerHTML=birthSummaryView(state,escape);}else $('#notificationText').textContent=decision?state.event.text:item.text;
$('#notificationChoices').innerHTML=decision?state.event.choices.map((c,i)=>`<button type="button" data-choice="${i}" class="primary">${escape(c.label)}</button>`).join(''):`<button type="button" id="ackNotification" class="primary">${queue.length>1?'Дальше':'Понятно'} <span>→</span></button>`;
if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();$('#notificationTitle').focus({preventScroll:true});dialog.scrollTop=0;}
function run(operation){const previousPartner=state.partnerId,previousMarriage=state.married;const before=state.log.length,unlocked=state.achievements.length;if(state.dev?.freeActions)state.energy=4;const error=operation();if(state.dev?.freeActions)state.energy=4;if(typeof error==='string'){render();const message=presentError(error);if($('#notification').open)$('#notificationError').textContent=message;else if($('#employmentDecision').open)$('#sideChoiceError').textContent=message;else toast(message);return;}state.money=Math.max(-1e12,Math.min(1e12,state.money));if(tab==='relations'){if(!previousMarriage&&state.married)relationsTab='family';else if(!previousPartner&&state.partnerId)relationsTab='partner';else if(previousPartner&&!state.partnerId&&relationsTab==='partner')relationsTab='family';}queue.push(...state.log.slice(before).filter(visibleNotice));if(state.achievements.length>unlocked)queue.push({title:'Новое достижение',kind:'achievement',text:state.achievements.slice(unlocked).map(a=>achievementDefinitions.find(d=>d.id===a.id)?.name).join(' · ')});const exhausted=exhaustionNotice(state);if(exhausted)queue.unshift(exhausted);render({persist:true});showNotification();}

function beginLife(random=false){const form=$('#createForm');if(!random&&!form.reportValidity())return;try{const gender=random?(Math.random()<0.5?'male':'female'):form.elements.namedItem('gender').value;const candidate=createLife(random?'':form.elements.namedItem('name').value,random?'random':form.elements.namedItem('country').value,gender);if(!random&&creationPreview){const error=applyCreationAppearance(candidate,creationPreview.appearance);if(error)throw Error(error);}else if(random){const a={...candidate.appearance,skin:skinTones[Math.floor(Math.random()*skinTones.length)].value};for(const key of ['hair','face','eyes','brows','nose','body']){const variants=Object.keys(appearanceOptions[key]).filter(value=>styleAllowed(gender,key,value));a[key]=variants[Math.floor(Math.random()*variants.length)];}applyCreationAppearance(candidate,a);}state=candidate;overviewOwner=null;developerUndo=null;developerSelected='';wardrobeGender=state.gender;hasSave=true;activeLife=true;tab='life';lifeMode='events';worldDraft.companions=[];relationsTab='family';queue=[];viewMemory.clear();renderedViewKey=null;document.body.classList.remove('at-start');$('#creation').close();render({persist:true});queue.push({title:'Рождение',text:state.log[0].text,birthSummary:true});showNotification();}catch(error){console.error(error);toast('Не удалось начать историю. Попробуй открыть свежий файл в браузере.');}}
$('#beginLife').addEventListener('click',()=>beginLife(false));$('#randomLife').addEventListener('click',()=>beginLife(true));$('#creation').addEventListener('close',()=>document.body.classList.remove('creating'));$('#createForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();beginLife();});
function downloadSave(){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(state,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}));const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='lifeline-save.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
$('#settingsButton').addEventListener('click',()=>{window.LifelineTheme?.sync();$('#developerButton').disabled=!activeLife;$('#settings').showModal();});
$('#closeSettings').addEventListener('click',()=>$('#settings').close());
$('#saveGame').addEventListener('click',()=>{save();downloadSave();toast('Файл сохранения скачан. Его можно открыть через «Загрузить игру».');});
$('#startNew').addEventListener('click',openCreation);
$('#startLoad').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!hasSave){$('#saveFile').click();return;}activeLife=true;document.body.classList.remove('at-start');const exhausted=exhaustionNotice(state);if(exhausted)queue.unshift(exhausted);render({persist:true});showNotification();});
render();if(loadWarning){$('#startWarning').hidden=false;$('#startWarning').textContent=loadWarning;$('#saveWarning').textContent=loadWarning;}

function previewImport(candidate){importCandidate=candidate;$('#importSummary').textContent=`${candidate.name} · ${ageText(candidate.age)} · ${profile(candidate).name}. Текущая жизнь будет заменена. Если браузер разрешает хранение, перед загрузкой создаётся копия для отмены.`;$('#importReview').showModal();}
$('#importSave').addEventListener('click',()=>$('#saveFile').click());
$('#saveFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{const file=e.target.files[0];if(!file)return;try{if(file.size>5*1024*1024)throw Error('Файл слишком большой: максимум 5 МБ.');const candidate=migrateLife(JSON.parse(await file.text()));if(!candidate)throw Error('Сохранение повреждено или формат не поддерживается. Текущая жизнь сохранена.');previewImport(candidate);}catch(error){if(error instanceof SyntaxError)error=new Error('Не удалось прочитать JSON. Выбери резервную копию игры.');if($('#creation').open)$('#saveWarning').textContent=error.message;else if(!activeLife){$('#startWarning').hidden=false;$('#startWarning').textContent=error.message;}else toast(error.message);}finally{e.target.value='';}});
$('#cancelImport').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#importReview').close();importCandidate=null;});
$('#confirmImport').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!importCandidate)return;const needsBackup=hasSave;let backupSaved=false;try{if(hasSave){localStorage.setItem('lifeline-backup-v1',JSON.stringify(state));backupSaved=true;}}catch{}state=importCandidate;overviewOwner=null;developerUndo=null;developerSelected='';wardrobeGender=state.gender;importCandidate=null;hasSave=true;activeLife=true;tab='life';lifeMode='events';worldDraft.companions=[];relationsTab='family';queue=[];viewMemory.clear();renderedViewKey=null;$('#importReview').close();if($('#creation').open)$('#creation').close();document.body.classList.remove('at-start');render({persist:true});if(state.event||state.pendingSideChoice)showNotification();toast(needsBackup&&!backupSaved?'Жизнь загружена. Браузер не позволил создать копию для отмены.':'Сохранение загружено.');});
$('#restoreBackup').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{const candidate=migrateLife(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('lifeline-backup-v1')));if(!candidate){toast('Резервная копия пока отсутствует. Она создаётся перед импортом.');return;}previewImport(candidate);}catch{toast('Не удалось прочитать резервную копию.');}});

document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.id!=='vacancyQuery')return;workFilters.query=e.target.value;render();});

document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{const current=event.target.closest('[role=tab]');if(!current||!['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Home','End'].includes(event.key))return;const tabs=Array.from(current.closest('[role=tablist]').querySelectorAll('[role=tab]'));const index=tabs.indexOf(current);const target=event.key==='Home'?0:event.key==='End'?tabs.length-1:(index+(event.key==='ArrowRight'?1:-1)+tabs.length)%tabs.length;event.preventDefault();tabs[target].focus({preventScroll:true});tabs[target].click();});

function updateGiftDescriptions(){for(const menu of document.querySelectorAll('.gift-menu')){const budget=Number(menu.querySelector('.gift-budget').value),preview=menu.querySelector('[data-gift-preview]');if(!preview)continue;preview.textContent=preview.dataset.romancePreview==='true'?'Обычный: '+giftName(budget,'normal')+'. Романтический: '+giftName(budget,'romantic')+'.':giftName(budget,'normal');}}

$('#employmentDecision').addEventListener('cancel',e=>e.preventDefault());document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.dataset.childHours)document.querySelector('#child-hours-'+e.target.dataset.childHours).textContent=e.target.value;});

document.title='Линия жизни · '+APP_VERSION;$('#appVersion').textContent=APP_VERSION;$('#releaseNotes').innerHTML=releaseHistory.map(r=>`<article><h3>${r.version} · ${r.title}</h3><ul>${r.changes.map(c=>'<li>'+escape(c)+'</li>').join('')}</ul></article>`).join('');

function renderCreationAppearance(){const gender=$('#createForm [name=gender]').value;if(!creationPreview){creationPreview={age:22,gender};initPersona(creationPreview);}creationPreview.gender=gender;for(const [key,options] of Object.entries(appearanceOptions))if(!styleAllowed(gender,key,creationPreview.appearance[key]))creationPreview.appearance[key]=Object.keys(options).find(value=>styleAllowed(gender,key,value));const wasOpen=$('#birthAppearance')?.open;$('#creationAppearance').innerHTML=creationAppearanceView(creationPreview);if(wasOpen)$('#birthAppearance').open=true;}
function updateFundingDescriptions(){for(const card of document.querySelectorAll('[data-level]')){const output=card.querySelector('[data-funding-preview]');if(!output)continue;const facultyId=card.querySelector('.faculty-select').value,f=prospectiveFunding(state,card.dataset.level,card.dataset.institution,facultyId,profile(state));output.textContent='Родители '+money(f.parents)+' · учебный кредит '+money(f.loan)+' · из личных денег '+money(f.student)+' за год. Направление: '+faculties[facultyId].name+'.';}}
renderCreationAppearance();

function updateBusinessQuotes(){for(const card of document.querySelectorAll('[data-business-start]')){const selected=card.querySelector('.business-premises')?.value,record=selected&&selected!=='rent'?estateRecord(state,selected):null,district=card.querySelector('.business-district');if(record)district.value=landById.get(record.plotId).district;district.disabled=!!record;const credit=card.querySelector('.business-credit').checked,q=businessStartQuote(state,card.dataset.businessStart,credit,record?.id,district.value),p=businessOpeningForecast(state,card.dataset.businessStart,credit,record?.id,district.value);card.querySelector('[data-start-price]').textContent='Старт '+money(q.total);card.querySelector('[data-start-reserve]').textContent=money(q.reserve);card.querySelector('[data-start-personal]').textContent=money(q.personal);card.querySelector('[data-start-loan]').textContent=money(Math.floor(q.total/2));card.querySelector('[data-start-profit]').textContent=money(p.profit);card.querySelector('[data-start-dividend]').textContent=money(p.dividend);card.closest('details.business-offer').querySelector('[data-offer-price]').textContent=money(q.total);for(const el of card.querySelectorAll('[data-start-cost]'))el.textContent=money(q.breakdown[el.dataset.startCost]);}}

function renderDeveloper(){const markup=developerUnlocked?developerView(state,{escape,money},developerSelected,!!developerUndo):'';const open=new Set([...$('#developerPanel').querySelectorAll('details[open]')].map(e=>e.id));$('#developerToolbar').hidden=!developerUnlocked||!activeLife;$('#developerLogin').hidden=developerUnlocked;$('#developerPanel').hidden=!developerUnlocked;if(developerUnlocked){$('#developerPanel').innerHTML=markup;for(const id of open)if(document.getElementById(id))document.getElementById(id).open=true;}$('#developerError').textContent='';}
function openDeveloper(){if(!activeLife)return;if($('#settings').open)$('#settings').close();try{renderDeveloper();if(!$('#developerDialog').open)$('#developerDialog').showModal();if(!developerUnlocked)$('#developerPassword').focus();}catch(error){console.error(error);$('#developerError').textContent='Не удалось открыть редактор. Сохрани жизнь и перезагрузи игру.';if(!$('#developerDialog').open)$('#developerDialog').showModal();}}
$('#developerButton').addEventListener('click',openDeveloper);$('#developerQuickOpen').addEventListener('click',openDeveloper);
$('#closeDeveloper').addEventListener('click',()=>$('#developerDialog').close());
function unlockDeveloper(){if(!developerPasswordMatches($('#developerPassword').value)){$('#developerError').textContent='Неверный пароль.';$('#developerPassword').value='';$('#developerPassword').focus();return;}try{developerUnlocked=true;$('#developerPassword').value='';renderDeveloper();$('#developerToolbar').hidden=false;$('#developerDialog').scrollTop=0;$('#developerTitle').focus();}catch(error){developerUnlocked=false;$('#developerLogin').hidden=false;$('#developerPanel').hidden=true;$('#developerToolbar').hidden=true;console.error(error);$('#developerError').textContent='Пароль принят, но редактор не смог прочитать текущую жизнь. Сохрани её и перезагрузи игру.';}}
$('#developerUnlock').addEventListener('click',unlockDeveloper);$('#developerLogin').addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();unlockDeveloper();});$('#developerPassword').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();unlockDeveloper();}});

function applyDeveloper(action){if(!developerUnlocked)return;if(action==='lock'){developerUnlocked=false;renderDeveloper();return;}if(action==='undo'){if(!developerUndo){$('#developerError').textContent='Нет правок для отмены.';return;}const restored=migrateLife(structuredClone(developerUndo));if(!restored){$('#developerError').textContent='Не удалось восстановить копию.';return;}state=restored;overviewOwner=null;developerUndo=null;queue=[];render({persist:true});renderDeveloper();toast('Последняя правка отменена.');return;}
 const values={};for(const input of $('#developerPanel').querySelectorAll('[data-dev-group]')){const group=input.dataset.devGroup,key=input.dataset.devKey;if(['character','job','person'].includes(group))values[key]=Number(input.value);else{values[group]??={};values[group][key]=Number(input.value);}}values.jobId=$('#devJobSelect')?.value;values.company=$('#devCompany')?.value;values.standard=$('#devStandardSalary')?.checked;values.key=$('#devPerson')?.value;values.relation=$('#devRelation')?.value||'keep';values.feelings??=0;values.playerFeelings??=0;values.enabled=$('#devFreeActions')?.checked;const result=developerEdit(state,action,values);if(result.error){$('#developerError').textContent=result.error;$('#developerError').scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'instant'});return;}developerUndo=structuredClone(state);try{localStorage.setItem('lifeline-dev-backup-v1',JSON.stringify(developerUndo));}catch{}state=result.state;overviewOwner=null;queue=[];renderedViewKey=null;viewMemory.clear();render({persist:true});renderDeveloper();toast('Параметры применены.');}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-dev-action]');if(b)applyDeveloper(b.dataset.devAction);});
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target.id==='devPerson'){developerSelected=e.target.value;renderDeveloper();}if(e.target.id==='devFreeActions')applyDeveloper('freeActions');});

})();
